\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c}|\gtrsim d^{-1/4-\Delta}

(91)

and

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(|\xi_{m,j,g}^{t}|+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa_{m,j,c}^{t}|\right)\lesssim 0.

(92)

Proof.

Let
t_{\Delta^{\prime}}
is the first time that
\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c}|=0
(note that we can take
t_{\Delta}
such that
\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t_{\Delta}}_{m,j,c}|\geq 0
while satisfying the conditions in
Lemma

B.12
and this gradient flow stops at
t=t_{\Delta^{\prime}}>t_{\Delta}
).
First, because
\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t_{\Delta}}_{m,j,c}|\geq 0
and the continuity of the derivative (except for
\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t_{\Delta}}_{m,j,c}|=0
), we have
\left.\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c}|\right|_{t=s}\geq 0
for all
s\in[t_{\Delta},t_{\Delta^{\prime}}]
. This implies that
\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c}|\gtrsim d^{-1/4-\Delta}
for all
t>t_{\Delta}
.

Next, we bound the derivative of
|\xi_{m,j,g}^{s}|+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{s}_{m,j,c}|
.
We assume that, there exists
t\in[t_{\Delta},\infty)
such that,
\sup_{t^{\prime}\in[t_{\Delta},t]}\left(|\xi_{m,j,g}^{t^{\prime}}|+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa_{m,j,c}^{t^{\prime}}|\right)\simeq\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2+\delta})
with
1/(6k^{*})>\delta>0
. However, for all
s\in[t_{\Delta},t]
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{sgn}(\xi_{m,j,g}^{s})\frac{d}{ds}\xi_{m,j,g}^{s}+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}\mathrm{sgn}(\kappa^{s}_{m,j,c})\frac{d}{ds}\kappa^{s}_{m,j,c}

(93)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{CJ}\left(\tilde{O}(\underbrace{(\alpha_{m,j,k^{*},1}^{t}-\alpha_{m,j,k^{*},2}^{t})}_{\lesssim\tilde{O}(1),\text{ {NOT assuming \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lemma-one-expert-coefficient-concrete}}}}|\xi_{m,j,g}|^{k^{*}-1}(1-(\xi_{mj1_{1}})^{2}))+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}\underbrace{\left(-\beta_{c,k^{*}}\alpha^{t}_{mjk^{*}{c}}\right)}_{\leq\tilde{O}(1)}(\kappa_{m,j,c})^{k^{*}}|\xi_{m,j,g}|\right.

(94)

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}\underbrace{\beta_{c,k^{*}}\alpha_{mjk^{*}{c}}^{t}}_{\geq\tilde{\Omega}(1)}\underbrace{(\kappa_{m,j,c})^{k^{*}}}_{{\gtrsim d^{-1/4-\Delta}}}|\xi_{m,j,g}|\right)+\frac{\eta}{CJ}\left(-\tilde{\Omega}\left(\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{s}_{m,j,c}|\right)^{k^{*}-1}+\underbrace{(\alpha_{m,j,k^{*},1}^{t}-\alpha_{m,j,k^{*},2}^{t})}_{\lesssim\tilde{O}(1),\text{{NOT assuming \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lemma-one-expert-coefficient-concrete}}}}\tilde{O}(|\xi_{m,j,g}|^{k^{*}})\right)

(95)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{CJ}|\xi_{m,j,g}|\tilde{O}\left({d^{-(k^{*}-2)(1/2-\delta)}-\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-k^{*}(1/4+\Delta)})}\right)

(96)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 0

(97)

where we used
-(k^{*}-2)(1/2-\delta)=-(k^{*}-2)(1/2-1/(6k^{*}))<k^{*}(1/4+1/(8k^{*}))=k^{*}(1/4+\Delta)
under the assumption that
k^{*}>4
(i.e.
k^{*}\geq 5
).
This contradicts with the assumption that
\left(|\xi_{m,j,g}^{t}|+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa_{m,j,c}^{t}|\right)\simeq(\mathrm{poly}\log d)d^{-1/2+\delta}
. Therefore, we have
\left(|\xi_{m,j,g}^{t}|+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa_{m,j,c}^{t}|\right)\lesssim(\mathrm{poly}\log d)d^{-1/2}
for all
t\in[t_{\Delta},\infty)
and this leads to
\mathrm{sgn}(\xi_{m,j,g}^{t})\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\xi_{m,j,g}^{t}+\sum_{c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}}\mathrm{sgn}(\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c})\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c}\lesssim 0
repeating the same calculation.
∎

By
Lemma

B.15
, we have the following lemma:

Lemma B.16
.

Assume
k^{*}>4
.
The following conditions hold true for all
t>t_{\Delta}
:

1.

\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}_{j}}|\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c}|\gtrsim d^{-1/4-1/(8k^{*})}
,

2.

|\kappa^{t}_{m,j,c}|\lesssim\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
for all
c\notin\mathcal{C}_{j}
,

3.

|\xi^{t}_{m,j,g}|\lesssim\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
for
c=1,2
,

Finally we have the following theorem by
Lemma

B.16
:

Theorem B.17
.

For all
j=1,\dots,J
,
c=1,2
, we have

\sup_{t\geq 0}|\xi^{t}_{m,j,g}|\lesssim\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})

(98)

with high probability.

Appendix C
Proof of MoE Training

In this section, we present a formal proof that the MoE can learn teacher models (
Theorem

4.6
), under
3.2
,
3.3
, and
3.4
. We execute the gradient-based optimization process outlined in Algorithm
1
. Our proof builds upon and extends the reasoning presented by
Oko et al. 2024a
. We introduce polylogarithmic constants
A_{i}
and
a_{i}
.
A_{i}
is of the order
\operatorname{polylog}(d)
, while
a_{i}
is of the order
\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}
, with the following order of strength:

\displaystyle{0<\>}\begin{aligned} &{A_{1}\lesssim{a_{2}}^{-1}\lesssim A_{2}\lesssim A_{4}\lesssim{a_{4}}^{-1}\lesssim A_{3}}\\
&{a_{2}}^{-1}\lesssim{a_{6}}^{-1}\lesssim A_{\rho}\lesssim A_{5},A_{6}\lesssim{a_{5}}^{-1}\end{aligned}=\tilde{O}(1).

A_{1}
is derived from the high-probability bounds on the gradient of the experts.

a_{2}
is derived from the threshold of the weak recovery of the neuron for the tasks.

A_{2}
and
A_{5}
are high-probability uniform bounds on the noise and small terms in the gradients of the experts and that of the gating network, respectively.

A_{3}
is derived from the upper bounds of the specific components in the gradients of the experts.

A_{4}
is derived from the upper bounds of the task correlation.

a_{4}
and
a_{5}
are derived from the upper bounds of the learning rates of the experts (
\eta_{e}
) and the router (
\eta_{r}
), respectively.

a_{6}
is derived from the sufficient step size in the optimization of the gating network.

A_{6}
originates from the lower bound of the Hermite coefficients in the router learning stage.

A_{\rho}
reflects the order of the mean vector scaling in terms of
\rho
, as given by
\rho\simeq A_{\rho}

An outline of the proof of MoE training is as follows:

1.

Initialization
(
Section

C.1
)

We first show that, after initialization, there exists a neuron
j^{*}_{m}
within the set of professional experts
\mathcal{M}_{c}
(
Definition

C.1
) corresponding to the task
c
that aligns with a constant factor stronger than other neurons (
Lemma

C.2
and
Corollary

C.3
).

2.

Exploration Stage
(
Section

C.2
)

After the exploration stage, the neuron
j^{*}_{m}
, which was strongly aligned during initialization, undergoes weak recovery for the task
c^{*}_{m}
it specializes in, whereas other neurons fail to achieve weak recovery and remains at a saddle point (
Lemma

C.6
).

3.

Router Learning Stage
(
Section

C.3
)

After the router learning stage, the router directs the data
x_{c}
from cluster corresponding to task
c
to the experts in
\mathcal{M}_{c}
(
Lemma

C.14
).

4.

Expert Learning Stage
(
Section

C.4
)

After the router completes its learning and experts are reinitialized, the experts
m
belonging to
\mathcal{M}_{c}
, which now receives the data
x_{c}
, achieve weak recovery (
Lemma

C.23
) without being affected by inter-cluster interference and subsequently attain strong recovery (
Lemma

C.28
).

5.

Second Layer Optimization Stage
(
Section

C.5
)

We finally show that by performing convex optimization on the second layer of the expert, the MoE achieves an
\epsilon
-error with respect to the teacher function (
Lemma

C.31
).

To establish
Theorem

4.6
, we apply a union bound over multiple events. Given that
M=O(1)
and
J\lesssim\sqrt{\log d}
, the set of events that hold with high probability remains closed under certain union bounds. By combining the aforementioned events, we conclude that the event in
Theorem

4.6
holds with probability at least
0.99
.

C.1
Initialization

To start off, we consider the initial alignment between the neurons and the index features. The following lemma shows that a constant fraction of the neurons aligns with the task of their corresponding cluster by a constant factor more strongly than the remaining neurons. We provide a definition of the set of professional experts that depends on initialization.

Definition C.1

(The set of the professional experts for class
c
)
.

(j_{m}^{*},c_{m}^{*})\coloneq\mathrm{argmax}_{j,c}{w_{m,j}}^{\top}w_{c}^{*},

(99)

\mathcal{M}_{c}\coloneq\{m\mid c=c_{m}^{*}\}.

(100)

The following Lemma holds with initialization.

Lemma C.2
.

Assume
C
is
O(1)
. Take arbitrary constants
\delta>0
and
A_{d}=1+O((\log d)^{-1})
.
If

M\simeq C\log(C/\delta)

(101)

and

\sqrt{\log d}\gtrsim J\gtrsim\frac{1}{C}\log\frac{M}{\delta},

(102)

then
|\mathcal{M}_{c}|\geq 1
for all
c
and

w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}}\geq\frac{2}{\sqrt{d}},\quad w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}}\geq\max_{c\neq c_{m}^{*}\text{ or }j\neq j_{m}^{*}}A_{d}|w_{m,j}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}\log d}

(103)

with probability at least
1-\delta
with sufficiently large
d
.

Proof.

Fix
m
. By the symmetry, we have

\mathbb{P}[|\mathcal{M}_{c}|\geq 1]\geq 1-(1-C^{-1})^{M}.

(104)

By union bound,

\mathbb{P}[|\mathcal{M}_{c}|\geq 1\;\forall c]\geq 1-M(1-C^{-1})^{M}\geq 1-M\exp(-M/C)\geq 1-\delta/3

(105)

where
M\simeq C\log(C/\delta)
.

w_{m,j}\sim\mathrm{Unif}(\mathbb{S}^{d-1}(1))
is obtained by
\frac{\tilde{w}_{m,j}}{\|\tilde{w}_{m,j}\|}
, where
\tilde{w}_{m,j}\sim N(0,\frac{1}{d}I)
. We have
\|\tilde{w}\|\sim 1
with high probability (the same argument as
Oko et al. 2024a
).
Consider the value of
\tilde{\kappa}_{m,j,c}=\tilde{w}_{m,j}^{\top}(I-\sum_{c^{\prime}\neq c}w_{c^{\prime}}^{*}{w_{c^{\prime}}^{*}}^{\top})w_{c}
and
\sum_{c^{\prime}\neq c}\tilde{w}_{m,j}^{\top}{w_{c^{\prime}}^{*}}(w_{c^{\prime}}^{*})^{\top}w_{c}
. Then, for each
\tilde{\kappa}_{m,j,c}
, there exists
\bar{\kappa}_{m,j,c}\overset{\text{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,d^{-1})
such that
\frac{\bar{\kappa}_{m,j,c}}{\tilde{\kappa}_{m,j,c}}=1+O(d^{-1/2})
because
\tilde{\kappa}_{m,j,c}
are independent and
\sum_{c^{\prime}\neq c}\tilde{w}_{m,j}^{\top}{w_{c^{\prime}}^{*}}(w_{c^{\prime}}^{*})^{\top}w_{c}^{*}\lesssim d^{-1}
.
Therefore, we evaluate the values of
\bar{\kappa}_{m,j,c}
instead of
w_{m,j}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}
.

We show that there is some
j
s.t.
\bar{\kappa}_{m,j,c}
is large enough: First,

\mathbb{P}[\bar{\kappa}_{m,j,c}<2d^{-1/2}]<0.9.

(106)

Then,

\mathbb{P}[\max_{c}\bar{\kappa}_{m,j,c}>2d^{-1/2}\;\text{for some $j$}]\geq 1-(0.9)^{JC}.

(107)

Taking

J\gtrsim\frac{1}{C}\log\frac{M}{\delta},

(108)

\mathbb{P}[\max_{c}\bar{\kappa}_{m,j,c}>2d^{-1/2}\;\text{for some $j$}]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{3M}.

(109)

Following
Chen et al. 2022
, we have

\left(1-\frac{\delta}{3MJ^{2}C^{2}}\right)w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}}\geq\max_{c\neq c_{m}^{*}\text{ or }j\neq j_{m}^{*}}|w_{mj}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}|-O(d^{-1})

(110)

and therefore

w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}}\geq\max_{c\neq c_{m}^{*}\text{ or }j\neq j_{m}^{*}}\left(1+\frac{\delta}{6MJ^{2}C^{2}}\right)|w_{m,j}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}|+\frac{\delta}{6MJ^{2}C^{2}}w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}}-O(d^{-1})

(111)

with probability at least
1-\delta/(3M)
and the desired result follows, using
\frac{\delta}{6MJ^{2}C^{2}}\gtrsim(\log d)^{-1}
and
w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}}\geq 2d^{-1/2}
.

∎

Since
w_{m,j}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}=O(\sqrt{\log d}/\sqrt{d})
with high probability and
\frac{\max_{c}{|\tilde{\alpha}^{0}_{m,j,k^{*},c}{\beta}_{c,k^{*}}|}}{\min_{c^{\prime}}|\tilde{\alpha}^{0}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}{\beta}_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}|}=1+\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
with high probability, by taking
\delta
as sufficiently small, we have the following inequality:

Corollary C.3

(Following
(
Chen et al. 2022
;
Oko et al. 2024a
)
)
.

When
J\gtrsim C^{-1}\log M
and
M\gtrsim C\log C
, for all
m
, we have at least one neuron
w_{m,j}
such that

\displaystyle w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}

and

\displaystyle|\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c_{m}^{*}}\beta_{c_{m}^{*},k^{*}}|{(w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}})}^{k^{*}-2}

\displaystyle\quad\geq\max\{\max_{j,c}|\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,k^{*},c}\beta_{c,k^{*}}|,|\max_{j}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\gamma_{k^{*}}|\}\max_{c\neq c_{m}^{*}\text{ or }j\neq j_{m}^{*}}{|w_{m,j}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}|}^{k^{*}-2}+a{(w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c_{m}^{*}})}^{k^{*}-2}.

with probability at least
0.999
, where
a
is a small constant
\lesssim{(\log d)}^{k^{*}-2}
.

Remark C.4
.

The term
\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,k^{*},c}
, defined in
Lemma

C.7
, varies with time
t
as it is a Hermite coefficient influenced by the mean vector
\rho v_{c}
of the data
x_{c}
. Nevertheless,
Corollary

C.3
holds for all
t
throughout the exploration stage. This is ensured by the bounds on the Hermite coefficients provided in
Lemma

C.8
and
Lemma

C.9
.

Remark C.5
.

From this section, we will discuss Phase I to IV on the event that the initialization was successful.

C.2
Exploration Stage

We train the first layer of the experts.
We employ the correlation loss to eliminate the interactions between neurons.
The alignment at time
t
, denoted as
\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}
, is defined as the inner product of the feature index
w^{*}_{c}
and the weight of
j
-th neuron
w^{t}_{m,j}
at time
t
, expressed as
\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}:={w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j}
. Similarly,
\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}:={w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j}
is defined in the same manner. The purpose of this subsection is to establish
Lemma

C.6
, which serves as the formal statement of
Lemma

4.9
. Within the expert set
\mathcal{M}_{c}
, there exists a neuron
j
such that the alignment magnitude
\kappa_{c,m,j}
with the feature index
w^{*}_{c}
satisfies
\kappa_{c,m,j}\geq a_{2}
for some constant
a_{2}>0
. In contrast, for all other expert sets
\mathcal{M}_{c^{\prime}}
with
c^{\prime}\neq c
, no neuron achieves such alignment; that is,
\kappa_{c^{\prime},m,j}\leq\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})<a_{2}
for all
j\in\mathcal{M}_{c^{\prime}}
. Moreover, the remaining neurons in
\mathcal{M}_{c}
also do not reach this level of alignment. To prove
Lemma

C.6
, we first decompose the stochastic gradient update into its population and noise components. Then, by introducing auxiliary sequences, we establish a lower bound for
{\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}
(i.e., weak recovery) and upper bounds for
\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert
for all
(c,j)\neq(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
and
\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert
for all
j\in[J]
.

Lemma C.6

(Formal)
.

Consider the expert
m\in\mathcal{M}_{c}
. Let
{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}w_{c^{\prime}}^{*}\leq A_{4}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
for all
c\neq c^{\prime}
,
{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}w_{g}^{*}\leq A_{4}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
for all
c
and
{\eta}^{t}=\eta_{e}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
. Then, with high probability, there exists some time
t_{1}\leq T_{1}=\tilde{\Theta}{({\eta_{e}}^{-1}d^{\frac{k^{*}-2}{2}})}
such that the following conditions hold:

•

{\kappa^{t_{1}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}\geq a_{2}
,

•

\lvert{\kappa^{t_{1}}_{c,m,j}}\rvert\leq 5A_{3}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
, for all
(c,j)\neq(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
,

•

\lvert{\kappa^{t_{1}}_{g,m,j}}\rvert\leq 5A_{3}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
, for all
j\in[J]
.

Gradient update decomposition.

First, we assess the evolution of the alignment by analyzing its population and stochastic contributions. Note that at the exploration stage, the router distributes the data to the experts with an equal probability of
\frac{1}{M}
because the weights
\theta_{m}
of the gating network are initialized to zero.

In
Lemma

C.7
, we will evaluate the update of the spherical gradient descent

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t+1}_{c,m,j}}={w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}{w^{t+1}_{c,m,j}}

\displaystyle={w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}\frac{\big[{w^{t}_{c,m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})\big]}{\big\|{{w^{t}_{m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\big\|}.

Note that
I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}
is a projection matrix used to project the gradient
\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})
onto the tangent space of the sphere
\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
. Additionally, normalization is performed to return the vector
w^{t+1}_{c,m,j}
to the unit sphere.

Lemma C.7
.

Suppose that
\eta^{t}=\eta_{e}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
and
\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}\geq\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}
. With high probability, the update of
\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}
and
\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}
satisfies the following bounds.

\displaystyle\kappa^{t+1}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}\geq{\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big[i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}

\displaystyle\quad+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}-{\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}}})}\Big]-{\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d

\displaystyle\quad+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{t}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top})}{{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}.

Let
c\neq c^{*}_{m}
or
j\neq j^{*}_{m}
, we have

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big[i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}})}\Big]-\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa^{t+1}_{c,m,j}\leq

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big[i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}})}\Big]+\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}.

Let
j\in[J]
, we have

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big[i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{(1-{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{2})}\Big]-\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{{(\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa^{t+1}_{g,m,j}\leq

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big[i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{(1-{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{2})}\Big]+\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}\rvert}{{\eta}^{t}}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}.

{{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}
represents a mean-zero random variable satisfying
\|{{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}\|=\tilde{O}(d^{\frac{1}{2}})
and
\lvert{{u}^{\top}{{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(1)
, where
u\sim\operatorname{Unif}{(\mathbb{S}^{d-1})}
, with high probability.
We can also obtain
\left|{\kappa^{t+1}_{c,m,j}-\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\right|=\tilde{O}(\eta_{e})
and
\left|{\kappa^{t+1}_{g,m,j}-\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\right|=\tilde{O}(\eta_{e})
with high probability.

Proof.

The population gradient for the first layer of the expert can be represented as a decomposition in the following manner.

\displaystyle{\nabla_{w_{m,j}}\mathbb{E}_{c}\mathbb{E}_{x_{c}}\Big[\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})\Big]}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{c}\mathbb{E}_{x_{c}}\Big[\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}{\sigma_{m}}^{\prime}({w_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})x_{c}\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c\in[C]}\mathbb{E}_{x_{c}}\Big[\Big(\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\beta_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}x_{c})+s_{c}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}x_{c})\Big)\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i-1}({w_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c})\Big)x_{c}\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\!\sum_{c\in[C]}\!\Big(\mathbb{E}_{z}\!\Big[\Big(\!\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\beta_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}z)\!+\!s_{c}\!\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}z)\Big)\!\Big(\!\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\sum_{l=0}^{i-1}\binom{i-1}{l}\mathrm{He}_{i-l-1}({w_{m,j}^{\top}z})(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l}\Big)z\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{z}\Big[\Big(\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\beta_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}z)+s_{c}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}z)\Big)\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\sum_{l=0}^{i-1}\binom{i-1}{l}\mathrm{He}_{i-l-1}({w_{m,j}^{\top}z})(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l}\Big)\rho v_{c}\Big]\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c\in[C]}\Big(\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{z}\Big[\Big(\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{i\beta_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i-1}({w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}z)\Big)\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\sum_{l=0}^{i-1}\binom{i-1}{l}\mathrm{He}_{i-l-1}({w_{m,j}^{\top}z})(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l}\Big)w_{c}^{*}\Big]}_{\text{(I)}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{z}\Big[\Big(s_{c}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{i\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i-1}({w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}z)\Big)\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\sum_{l=0}^{i-1}\binom{i-1}{l}\mathrm{He}_{i-l-1}({w_{m,j}^{\top}z})(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l}\Bigg)w_{g}^{*}\Big]}_{\text{(II)}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{z}\Big[\!\Big(\!\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\beta_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}z)\!\!+\!\!s_{c}\!\!\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}z)\Big)\!\!\Big(\!\sum_{i=2}^{\infty}\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\sum_{l=0}^{i-2}\binom{i-1}{l}(i-l-1)\mathrm{He}_{i-l-2}({w_{m,j}^{\top}z})(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l}\Big)w_{m,j}\!\Big]}_{\text{(III)}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{z}\Big[\Big(\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\beta_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}z)+s_{c}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathrm{He}_{i}({w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}z)\Big)\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}\sum_{l=0}^{i-1}\binom{i-1}{l}\mathrm{He}_{i-l-1}({w_{m,j}^{\top}z})(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l}\Big)\rho v_{c}\Big]}_{\text{(IV)}}\Big).

where the second equality is due to
\mathbb{P}{(m(x_{c})=m)}=\frac{1}{M}
and
\pi_{m(x_{c})}=\frac{1}{M}
. The third equality follows from the definition
x_{c}:=z+\rho v_{c}
where
z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})
, along with the condition
{v_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}=0
and
{v_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}=0
for all
(c,c^{\prime})
, as well as the binomial expansion. The fourth equality is due to Stein’s Lemma and integration by parts.

With the spherical gradient,
(III)
is negligible since
w_{m,j}
is unit-norm and
{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w_{m,j}{w_{m,j}}^{\top})w_{m,j}=0
. When considering
{v_{c}}^{\top}\nabla_{w_{m,j}}\mathbf{E}[\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})]
,
(IV)
can be ignored because
{v_{c}}^{\top}w_{c^{\prime}}^{*}=0
for all
(c,c^{\prime})
. Thus, we expand
(I)
and
(II)
.

(I)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\sum_{l=i}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l-i}\Big)\frac{i\beta_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w_{m,j})}^{i-1}{w^{*}_{c}}.

(II)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\sum_{l=i}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho w_{m,j}^{\top}v_{c})^{l-i}\Big)\frac{is_{c}\gamma_{c,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w_{m,j})}^{i-1}{w^{*}_{g}}.

Here, we introduce the discrepancy
{\Xi^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}
between the population and the empirical gradient.

\displaystyle{{\Xi^{t}}_{w_{m},j}=}

\displaystyle{-\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}

\displaystyle{+\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{E}[\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})]}

We evaluate the empirical update of the alignment.

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t+1}_{c,m,j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}{\big\|{{w^{t}_{m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\big\|}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\geq}{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert{(\eta^{t})}^{2}}{2}{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{(\eta^{t})}^{3}}{2}{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{3}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\geq}{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\sum_{l=i}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c^{\prime}})^{l-i}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{i\beta_{c^{\prime},i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\sum_{l=i}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c^{\prime}})^{l-i}\Big)\frac{is_{c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{g}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle={\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c}^{\prime})^{l-i}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{i\beta_{c^{\prime},i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c^{\prime}})^{l-i}\Big)\frac{is_{c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{g}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle={\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(i\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}})}\Big)-\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}.

where (i) is due to Taylor expansion, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and the orthogonality property of the Hermite polynomials. In (ii), we used the expansion of (I) and (II) and
\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|\leq A_{1}d^{\frac{1}{2}}
which holds with high probability. Here, we introduced
\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}
defined by

\displaystyle\frac{i{\tilde{\alpha}}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}}{\sqrt{i!}}:=\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}{(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}\top v_{c})}^{l-i}\Big).

Furthermore, it can be equivalently rewritten as follows:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\alpha}}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}^{\prime}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}z+\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}}+b_{m,j})\mathrm{He}_{i}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}z)\right].

Note that the definition of
\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c}
given here differs from that of
\alpha_{m,j,i,c}
in Appendix
B
. In Appendix
C
, we define the Hermite coefficients by incorporating the perturbation induced by
\rho{w_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c}
, and formulate them in a manner involving a first-order derivative. To distinguish this modified definition, we introduced the tilde notation.

In the same way, we obtain an upper bound as follows:

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t+1}_{c,m,j}}

\displaystyle\leq{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(i\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}-{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}})}\Big)+\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+(I_{d}-{w^{t}_{m,j}}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}.

When
(c,j)=(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
,
\frac{{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}\leq{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d
since we have
\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}\geq d^{-\frac{1}{2}}
and
\eta^{t}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
.

We obtain an upper bound on the difference of
\kappa_{c,m,j}
over a single step.

\displaystyle\lvert{\kappa^{t+1}_{c,m,j}-\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert

\displaystyle{\leq{\eta}^{t}\lvert{{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad{+\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}{(\eta^{t})}^{2}}{2}{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad{+\frac{(\eta^{t})^{3}}{2}\lvert{{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad{\cdot{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\tilde{O}(\eta_{e}).

All terms on the RHS are bounded by
\tilde{O}(\eta_{e})
since

\lvert{{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\rvert=\tilde{O}(1)
,

{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}=\tilde{O}(d)
, and

\lvert{{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}}\rvert\\
\quad\cdot{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}=\tilde{O}(d)
with high probability.

We establish similar statements for
\kappa_{g,m,j}
. We obtain a lower bound as follows:

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t+1}_{g,m,j}}

\displaystyle={\frac{{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}{w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}{\big\|{{w^{t}_{m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\big\|}}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\geq}{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}-{\eta}^{t}{w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert{(\eta^{t})}^{2}}{2}{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{(\eta^{t})}^{3}}{2}{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{3}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\geq}{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\sum_{l=i}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c^{\prime}})^{l-i}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{i\beta_{c^{\prime},i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\sum_{l=i}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c^{\prime}})^{l-i}\Big)\frac{is_{c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{g}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle={\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c}^{\prime})^{l-i}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{i\beta_{c^{\prime},i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}+{\eta}^{t}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{1}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c^{\prime}})^{l-i}\Big)\frac{is_{c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}}{\sqrt{i!}}(i-1)!{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{t}_{m,j})}^{i-1}\Big){w^{*}_{g}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle={\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(i\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{(1-{({\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}})}^{2})}\Big)-\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}.

where (i) is due to Taylor expansion, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and the orthogonality property of the Hermite polynomials. In (ii), we used the expansion of (I) and (II) and
{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}\leq A_{1}d^{\frac{1}{2}}
which holds with high probability.

In the same way, we obtain an upper bound as follows:

\displaystyle{\kappa^{t+1}_{g,m,j}}

\displaystyle\leq{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}+\frac{{\eta}^{t}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(i\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}{({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}+i{s_{c^{\prime}}}\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot{(1-{({\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}})}^{2})}\Big)-\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta^{t}}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}.

Similar to
\kappa_{c,m,j}
, we obtain an upper bound on the difference of
\kappa_{g,m,j}
over a single step.

\displaystyle\lvert{\kappa^{t+1}_{g,m,j}-\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert

\displaystyle{\leq{\eta}^{t}\lvert{{w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top})\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad{+\frac{{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}{(\eta^{t})}^{2}}{2}{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad{+\frac{(\eta^{t})^{3}}{2}\lvert{{w_{g}^{*}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{t}_{m,j}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad{\cdot{\|{\nabla_{w^{t}_{m,j}}\mathbf{1}\left(m(x_{c})=m\right)\pi_{m}(x_{c})y_{c}a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}x_{c}+b_{m,j})}\|}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\tilde{O}(\eta_{e}).

∎

Note that
\Xi^{t}_{w_{m,j}}
are mean-zero sub-Weibull random variables, and their partial sums exhibit strong concentration behavior.

Weak recovery for the corresponding cluster.

Building on Lemma
C.7
, we establish
Lemma

C.6
.

Now we show that the mean vector
\rho v_{c}
does not significantly alter the Hermite coefficients of the activation function when
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Lemma C.8
.

Suppose that
\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}w^{s}_{m,j}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
,
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
and
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t\leq\tilde{O}(d^{k^{*}-1})
. Then, by setting
{\eta}^{t}=\eta_{e}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
, we obtain that
\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}^{t+1}_{m,j,i,c}-\alpha_{m,j,i}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
with high probability.

Proof.

Consider the case where
\alpha_{m,j,i}>0
.
Suppose that
\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
,
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
and
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t
. Then, by leveraging the evaluation of the gradient update in
Lemma

C.7
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{t+1}_{m,j}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{t}_{m,j}}\rvert+\lvert{\frac{\eta^{t}\rho}{CM^{2}}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\sqrt{i+1}\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}\beta_{c,i}{(\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j})}^{i}(1-{({v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{t}_{m,j}})}^{2})}\rvert

(112)

\displaystyle\quad{+\lvert{\frac{\eta^{t}\rho}{CM^{2}}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\sqrt{i+1}s_{c}\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j})}^{i}(1-{({v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{t}_{m,j}})}^{2})}\rvert}

(113)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{{\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{t}_{m,j}}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{t})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{t})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta}^{t}{v_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-{w^{t}_{m,j}}{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{t}}_{w_{m,j}}

(114)

\displaystyle{\leq\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{0}_{m,j}}\rvert+\sum_{s=0}^{t}\Big[\frac{\eta^{s}\rho}{CM^{2}}p^{*}\sqrt{p^{*}+1}(\max_{i}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\beta_{c,i}}\rvert}{(\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j})}^{k^{*}}+\max_{i}{\lvert{s_{c}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{k^{*}})}

(115)

\displaystyle\quad{+\frac{{\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert}{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta}^{s}{v_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-{w^{s}_{m,j}}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w^{s}_{m,j}}\Big]}

(116)

\displaystyle{\leq\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{0}_{m,j}}\rvert+t\frac{\eta_{e}\rho}{CM^{2}}p^{*}\sqrt{p^{*}+1}(\max_{i,s}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\beta_{c,i}}\rvert}\max_{s}{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert}^{k^{*}}+\max_{i,s}{\lvert{s_{c}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}\max_{s}{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert}^{k^{*}})}

(117)

\displaystyle\quad{+t\frac{\max_{s}{\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert}{({\eta}_{e})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}_{e})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+\sum_{s=0}^{t}{\eta_{e}}{v_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-{w^{s}_{m,j}}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w^{s}_{m,j}}}

(118)

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})

(119)

where the last inequality is due to
\frac{\eta_{e}\rho}{CM^{2}}p^{*}\sqrt{p^{*}+1}(\max_{i,s}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\beta_{c,i}}\rvert}\max_{s}{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert}^{k^{*}}+\max_{i,s}{\lvert{s_{c}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}\max_{s}{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert}^{k^{*}})=\tilde{O}(d^{-k^{*}})
,

\frac{\max_{s}{\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert}{({\eta}_{e})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}=\tilde{O}(d^{{-k^{*}}+\frac{1}{2}})
, and
\frac{{({\eta}_{e})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}=\tilde{O}(d^{{-\frac{3k^{*}}{2}}+\frac{3}{2}})
, since we have
\eta_{e}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
,
\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
, and
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.
Note that
\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{0}_{m,j}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
with high probability.

Additionally, when
t\leq\tilde{O}(d^{k^{*}-1})
,

\displaystyle\lvert\sum_{s=0}^{t}{\eta_{e}}{v_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-{w^{s}_{m,j}}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(\eta_{e}\sqrt{t})=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})

with high probability.

Recall that

\displaystyle\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}{(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}\top v_{c^{\prime}})}^{l-i}\Big)=\frac{i\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{i!}}.

Since
\rho{v_{c}}^{\top}w^{s}_{m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
, the series
\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}{(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c^{\prime}})}^{l-i}\Big)
decays exponentially with respect to
d
.
Together with
{v_{c}}^{\top}w^{0}_{m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
and
\kappa^{0}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
with high probability over initialization randomness, we obtain
\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}^{t+1}_{m,j,i,c}-\alpha_{m,j,i}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
with high probability by induction.
The proof can be similarly established for the case where
\alpha_{m,j,i}<0
since
k^{*}
is even.
∎

Furthermore, we demonstrate that when
\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}
grows asymptotically larger than those of other neurons whose
\kappa^{t}_{c,m,j}
have not increased significantly,
{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{t}_{m,j}}
also becomes larger, leading to an increase in the Hermite coefficient
\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}
.

Lemma C.9
.

Consider a neuron which satisfies
\alpha_{m,j,k^{*}}\beta_{c,k^{*}}>0
and
\alpha_{m,j,i}\beta_{c,i}>0
for
k^{*}<i\leq p^{*}
. Suppose that
\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}w^{s}_{m,j}\rvert=\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
,
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
, and
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t\leq\tau=\tilde{O}(d^{k^{*}-1})
. Then, by setting
{\eta}^{t}=\eta_{e}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
, we obtain
\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}^{t+1}_{m,j,i,c}}\rvert-\lvert{\alpha_{m,j,i}}\rvert=\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
with high probability.

Proof.

Consider the case where
\alpha_{m,j,i}>0
. Suppose that
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})\geq 0
and
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t
, we have

\displaystyle{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{t+1}_{m,j}}

\displaystyle\geq{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{0}_{m,j}}+\frac{\eta_{e}\rho}{CM^{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}\Big[{\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\sqrt{i+1}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\beta_{c,i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j})}^{i}(1-{({v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}})}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\sqrt{i+1}s_{c}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{i}(1-{({v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}})}^{2})\Big]

\displaystyle\quad-t\frac{{\max_{s}{\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert}}{({\eta}_{e})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-t\frac{{({\eta}_{e})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+\sum_{s=0}^{t}{\eta_{e}}{v_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-{w^{s}_{m,j}}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle\geq{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{0}_{m,j}}+\frac{\eta_{e}\rho}{CM^{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}{\sqrt{k^{*}+1}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,k^{*},c}\beta_{c,k^{*}}{(\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j})}^{i}(1-{({v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}})}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta_{e}\rho}{CM^{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}p^{*}\sqrt{p^{*}+1}\max_{c}{\lvert s_{c}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\gamma_{i}\rvert}{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert}^{k^{*}}(1-{({v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}})}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad-t\frac{{\max_{s}{\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert}}{({\eta}_{e})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-t\frac{{({\eta}_{e})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}-\Big\lvert{\sum_{s=0}^{t}{\eta_{e}}{v_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-{w^{s}_{m,j}}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}}\Big\rvert

\displaystyle=\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

where we used
\frac{\eta_{e}\rho}{CM^{2}}p^{*}\sqrt{p^{*}+1}\max_{c}{\lvert s_{c}\tilde{\alpha}^{s}_{m,j,i,c}\gamma_{i}\rvert}{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert}^{k^{*}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-k^{*}})
,

\frac{\max_{s}{\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert}{({\eta}_{e})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}=\tilde{O}(d^{{-k^{*}}+\frac{1}{2}})
, and
\frac{{({\eta}_{e})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}=\tilde{O}(d^{{-\frac{3k^{*}}{2}}+\frac{3}{2}})
, since we have
\eta_{e}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
,
\lvert{{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{s}_{m,j}}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
, and
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
. In addition, when
t\leq\tilde{O}(d^{k^{*}-1})
, we have that
\lvert\sum_{s=0}^{t}{\eta_{e}}{v_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-{w^{s}_{m,j}}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(\eta_{e}\sqrt{t})=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
with high probability.

Note that
\lvert{v_{c}}^{\top}{w^{0}_{m,j}}\rvert=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
with high probability.
Thus, combined with the assumption that
\text{sgn}(\alpha_{m,j,i})
is the same for all
i
and
\rho=\tilde{O}(1)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{i\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}}{\sqrt{i!}}=\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\underbrace{\sum_{l=i+1}^{\infty}\Big(\frac{l\alpha_{m,j,l}}{\sqrt{l!}}\binom{l-1}{l-i}{(\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}\top v_{c})}^{l-i}\Big)}_{=\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})}=\frac{i\alpha_{m,j,i}}{\sqrt{i!}}+\tilde{\Omega}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

The same holds even when
\alpha_{m,j,i}<0
by considering the upper bound in the same manner.
∎

We show that even as
w^{t}_{m,j}{}^{\top}v_{c}
increases, the coefficient
\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{c,m,j,i}
remains bounded by a polylogarithmic function in
d
.

Lemma C.10
.

\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[a_{m,j,i}\sigma_{m}^{\prime}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}z+\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}})\mathrm{He}_{i}({w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}z)\right]=\tilde{O}(1)
, where
z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})
and
\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}}=\tilde{O}(1)
.

Proof.

Define
Z:={w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, since
\|w^{t}_{m,j}\|=1
.
Also note that
\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}v_{c}=\tilde{O}(1)
, given
\|v_{c}\|=1
and
\rho=\tilde{O}(1)
.
By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}^{\prime}(Z+\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}}+b_{m,j})\mathrm{He}_{i}(Z)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{Z}[{a_{m,j}}^{2}{\sigma_{m}^{\prime}(Z+\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}}+b_{m,j})}^{2}]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{Z}[\mathrm{He}_{i}(Z)^{2}]}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{i!}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{Z}[{a_{m,j}}^{2}{\sigma_{m}^{\prime}(Z+\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}}+b_{m,j})}^{2}]}.

We now provide casewise bounds according to the activation function
\sigma_{m}
.

If
\sigma_{m}(\cdot)
is the ReLU function
\sigma_{m}(\cdot)=\max{(0,\cdot)}
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{Z}[{a_{m,j}}^{2}{\sigma_{m}^{\prime}(Z+\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}}+b_{m,j})}^{2}]}\leq\lvert a_{m,j}\rvert.

If
\sigma_{m}(\cdot)
is a degree-
p
polynomial
\sigma_{m}^{\prime}(\cdot+b_{m,j})=\sum_{q=0}^{p-1}C_{q}(\cdot)^{q}
with
p=O(1)
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[a_{m,j}^{2}\sigma_{m}^{\prime}(Z+\rho w^{t}_{m,j}{}^{\top}v_{c}+b_{m,j})^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle=|a_{m,j}|\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{Z}\Big[\big(\sum_{q=0}^{p-1}C_{q}(Z+\rho w^{t}_{m,j}{}^{\top}v_{c})^{q}\big)^{2}\Big]}

\displaystyle\leq|a_{m,j}|\sqrt{\sum_{q=0}^{p-1}\sum_{r=0}^{p-1}|C_{q}C_{r}|\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[(Z+\rho w^{t}_{m,j}{}^{\top}v_{c})^{q+r}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq|a_{m,j}|\sqrt{\sum_{u=0}^{2(p-1)}C_{u}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[(Z+\rho w^{t}_{m,j}{}^{\top}v_{c})^{u}\right]}

\displaystyle=|a_{m,j}|\sqrt{\sum_{u=0}^{2(p-1)}C_{u}\sum_{j=0}^{u}\binom{u}{j}\mathbb{E}[Z^{j}]{\lvert\rho w^{t}_{m,j}{}^{\top}v_{c}\rvert}^{u-j}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(1)|a_{m,j}|

where the constants
C_{q}
,
C_{r}
, and
C_{u}
arise from the binomial expansion.
Combining both cases, we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{c,m,j,i}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{i!}}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[a_{m,j}\sigma_{m}^{\prime}(Z+\rho{w^{t}_{m,j}}^{\top}{v_{c}}+b_{m,j})\mathrm{He}_{i}(Z)\right]=\tilde{O}(1)

as desired.
∎

Remark C.11
.

For the sake of conciseness in the exposition of the proof, we omit the superscript
t
in
\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{m,j,i,c}
. Based on
Lemma

C.8
,
Lemma

C.9
, and
Lemma

C.10
, the bounds in the subsequent lemmas are properly justified, regardless of the variations in the coefficients
\tilde{\alpha}^{t}_{c,m,j,i}
.

To prove
Lemma

C.6
, We introduce auxiliary sequences that provide the following bounds.

Lemma C.12
.

Consider the expert
m\in\mathcal{M}_{c}
. Let
{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}w_{c^{\prime}}^{*}\leq A_{4}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
for all
c\neq c^{\prime}
,
{w_{c}^{*}}^{\top}w_{g}^{*}\leq A_{4}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-1/2})
for all
c
, and
{\eta}^{t}=\eta_{e}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
. For all
s=0,1,\ldots,t
, suppose that

•

\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}\leq a_{2}
,

•

\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert\leq\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}
for all
(c,j)\neq(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
,

•

\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert\leq A_{2}A_{3}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}
for all
(c,j)\neq(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
,

•

\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert\leq\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}
for all
j
,

•

\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert\leq A_{2}A_{3}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}
for all
j
.

Then, by introducing auxiliary sequences
(P^{s}_{\text{I}})_{s=0}^{t+1}
and
(Q^{s}_{\text{I}})_{s=0}^{t+1}
characterized as follows:

\displaystyle P^{s+1}_{\text{I}}

\displaystyle=P^{s}_{\text{I}}+\frac{\eta^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{(P^{s}_{\text{I}})}^{k^{*}-1}\text{ with }P^{0}_{\text{I}}=(1-a_{2})\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}

and

\displaystyle Q^{s+1}_{\text{I}}=Q^{s}_{\text{I}}

\displaystyle+(1+a_{2})\frac{\eta^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\max\Big\{\max_{c^{\prime}}\big\lvert\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}\big\rvert,\big\lvert\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\gamma_{k^{*}}\big\rvert\Big\}{(Q^{s}_{\text{I}})}^{k^{*}-1}

\displaystyle+A_{3}\frac{\eta^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{(\kappa^{s+1}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{ with }Q^{0}_{\text{I}}=(1+a_{2})\max\{{\max_{c}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert},\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert},\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\},

\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}
is lower bounded by
P^{s}_{\text{I}}
for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t+1
with high probability.
For all
(c,j)\neq(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
,
\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert
and
\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert
are upper bounded by
Q^{s}_{\text{I}}
for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t+1
with high probability.

Proof.

Suppose that
{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}\leq a_{2}
,
\lvert{{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}}\rvert\leq{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}
for all
c\neq c_{m}^{*}\text{ or }j\notin\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
, and
\lvert{{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}}\rvert\leq A_{2}A_{3}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}
for all
j\in[J]
for all
s=0,1,...,t
.

We first deduce the auxiliary sequence
(P^{s}_{\text{I}})_{s=0}^{t+1}
.

\displaystyle{\kappa^{s+1}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}

\displaystyle\geq{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big[i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\beta_{c,i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{i-1}({w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}-{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j^{*}_{m}}})

\displaystyle\quad+i{s_{c}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{i-1}({w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}-{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}}})\Big]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}-\frac{{{\eta}^{s}}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}-{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w^{s}_{m,j}}

\displaystyle\geq{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}(1-{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{2}){{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\eta}^{s}{p^{*}}^{2}}{M^{2}}\max_{c,i}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\beta_{c,i}}\rvert}\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j^{*}_{m}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}}\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\eta}^{s}{p^{*}}^{2}}{M^{2}}\max_{c,i}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\beta_{c,i}}\rvert}\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j^{*}_{m}}\rvert}^{k^{*}}}-\frac{{\eta}^{s}{p^{*}}^{2}}{M^{2}}\max_{c}{\lvert{s_{c}}\rvert}\max_{i,c}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}}\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\eta}^{s}{p^{*}}^{2}}{M^{2}}\max_{c}{\lvert{s_{c}}\rvert}\max_{i,c}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}}\rvert}^{k^{*}}}-{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{{A_{1}}^{2}}d+{\eta}^{s}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}{(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top})}{{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}.

\displaystyle\geq{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}(1-{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{2}){{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\eta}^{s}{p^{*}}^{2}}{M^{2}}\max\left\{\max_{c,i}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\beta_{c,i}}\rvert},\max_{c}{\lvert{s_{c}}\rvert}\max_{i,c}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}\right\}{{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot(\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}}\rvert}+\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j^{*}_{m}}}\rvert}+\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}}\rvert}+{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}}}\rvert})

\displaystyle\quad-{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{{\eta}^{s}}a{{A_{1}}^{2}}{d^{-\frac{k^{*}-2}{2}}}+{\eta}^{s}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}{(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top})}{{\Xi}^{s}}_{w^{s}_{m,j}}.

We used conditions
{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
c\neq c^{\prime}
and
{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
c
,
{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{2}\leq{a_{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{4}a_{2}
,
\frac{{\eta}^{s}{p^{*}}^{2}}{M^{2}}\max\left\{\max_{c,i}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\beta_{c,i}}\rvert},\max_{c}{\lvert{s_{c}}\rvert}\max_{i,c}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},i,c}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}\right\}{{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}(\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}}\rvert}+\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j^{*}_{m}}}\rvert}+\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}}\rvert}+{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j^{*}_{m}}}\rvert})\leq\frac{{a_{2}\eta}^{s}}{4CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}
, and
{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}{{\eta}^{s}}a_{4}{{A_{1}}^{2}}{d^{-\frac{k^{*}-2}{2}}}\leq\frac{{a_{2}\eta}^{s}}{4CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}.

Hence,

\displaystyle{\kappa^{s+1}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}

\displaystyle\geq{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}+(1-\frac{3}{4}a_{2})\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{{\left(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}\right)}^{k^{*}-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+{\eta}^{s}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}{(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{s}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top})}{{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle\geq{\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\Big[(1-\frac{3}{4}a_{2})\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{{\left(\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}\right)}^{k^{*}-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}{(I_{d}-w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top})}{{\Xi}^{s^{\prime}}}_{w_{m,j}}\Big].

We bound the noise term. Note that
\Xi^{s}_{w_{m,j}}
has a sub-Weibull tail.

If
s\leq A_{2}(\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})^{2-2k^{*}}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}{(I_{d}-w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top})}{{\Xi}^{s^{\prime}}}_{w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j}}\leq{\eta_{e}}A_{1}\sqrt{s}\leq a_{4}A_{1}\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}\leq a_{2}\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}

with high probability.

If
s>A_{2}(\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})^{2-2k^{*}}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}^{\top}{(I_{d}-w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}{w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j^{*}_{m}}}^{\top})}{{\Xi}^{s^{\prime}}}_{w_{m,j}}

\displaystyle\leq{\eta}^{s}A_{1}s^{-\frac{1}{2}}s\leq\eta_{e}sA_{1}A_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{(\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{a_{2}\eta_{e}}}{4CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{{(\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}

with high probability.

Therefore, for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t
,
{\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}
can be lower bounded as

\displaystyle{\kappa^{s+1}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}

\displaystyle\geq(1-a_{2}){\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{(1-a_{2})\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{{(\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}}.

With the aid of an auxiliary sequence
\left(P^{s}_{\text{I}}\right)_{s=0}^{t+1}
, where
P^{0}_{\text{I}}=(1-a_{2})\kappa^{0}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}
, and

\displaystyle P^{s+1}_{\text{I}}

\displaystyle=P^{s}_{\text{I}}+\frac{\eta^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j^{*}_{m},k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{(P^{s}_{\text{I}})}^{k^{*}-1},

\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}}
is lower bounded by
P^{s}_{\text{I}}
for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t+1
.

Next, we deduce the auxiliary sequence
(Q^{s}_{\text{I}})_{s=0}^{t+1}
.

For the upper bound of
\max_{c\neq c_{m}^{*}\text{ or }j\notin\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s+1}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}
, we have

{\lvert{\kappa^{s+1}_{c,m,j}-\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}\leq A_{1}{\eta_{e}}
with high probability. Thus, the sign of
\kappa^{s+1}_{c,m,j}
is the same as that of
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}
, or
\lvert{\kappa^{s+1}_{c,m,j}}\rvert\leq A_{1}{\eta_{e}}
. Similarly, the sign of
\kappa^{s+1}_{g,m,j}
is the same as that of
\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}
, or
\lvert{\kappa^{s+1}_{g,m,j}}\rvert\leq A_{1}{\eta_{e}}
.

Fix
m\in[M]
.

We show that the following bounds hold for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t+1
.

•

\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}
is upper bounded by
Q^{s}_{\text{I},c}
for all
j\in\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
,

where the sequence
\left(Q^{s}_{\text{I},c}\right)_{s=0}^{t+1}
is defined recursively as follows:

\displaystyle Q^{0}_{\text{I},c}=(1+a_{2})\max\{{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert},\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\},

and for
s\geq 0
,

\displaystyle Q^{s+1}_{\text{I},c}=Q^{s}_{\text{I},c}+(1+a_{2})\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\max_{c^{\prime}}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}}\rvert}{(Q^{s}_{\text{I},c})}^{k^{*}-1}+A_{3}\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}},

with high probability.

•

\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert
is upper bounded by
Q^{s}_{\text{I},g}
for all
j\in\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
,

where the sequence
\left(Q^{s}_{\text{I},g}\right)_{s=0}^{t+1}
is defined recursively as follows:

\displaystyle Q^{0}_{\text{I},g}=(1+a_{2})\max\{{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert},\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\},

and for
s\geq 0
,

\displaystyle Q^{s+1}_{\text{I},g}=Q^{s}_{\text{I},g}+(1+a_{2})\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}{s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\gamma_{k^{*}}}}\rvert}{(Q^{s}_{\text{I},g})}^{k^{*}-1}+A_{3}\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}},

with high probability.

•

\max_{c\in[C]}\lvert{{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}}\rvert
is upper bounded by
R^{s}_{\text{I},c}
for all
j\notin\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
,

where the sequence
\left(R^{s}_{\text{I},c}\right)_{s=0}^{t+1}
is defined recursively as follows:

\displaystyle R^{0}_{\text{I},c}=(1+a_{2})\max\{\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert},\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\},

and for
s\geq 0
,

\displaystyle R^{s+1}_{\text{I},c}=R^{s}_{\text{I},c}+(1+a_{2})\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\max_{c^{\prime}}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}}\rvert}{(R^{s}_{\text{I},c})}^{k^{*}-1},

with high probability.

•

\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert
is upper bounded by
R^{s}_{\text{I},g}
for all
j\notin\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
,

where the sequence
\left(R^{s}_{\text{I},g}\right)_{s=0}^{t+1}
is defined recursively as follows:

\displaystyle R^{0}_{\text{I},g}=(1+a_{2})\max\{{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert},\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\},

and for
s\geq 0
,

\displaystyle R^{s+1}_{\text{I},g}=R^{s}_{\text{I},g}+(1+a_{2})\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}{s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\gamma_{k^{*}}}}\rvert}{(R^{s}_{\text{I},g})}^{k^{*}-1},

with high probability.

Furthermore, for all
s=0,1,\ldots,t+1
,
Q^{s}_{\text{I},c}
,
Q^{s}_{\text{I},g}
,
R^{s}_{\text{I},c}
, and
R^{s}_{\text{I},g}
are upper bounded by
Q^{s}_{\text{I}}
.

We sequentially present each bound.

For
c\neq c^{*}_{m}
and
j\in\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
,

\displaystyle\lvert{\kappa^{s+1}_{c,m,j}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\Big\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big(i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}\Big({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}s_{c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}\Big({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}-{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\Big)\Big)+\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert{{\eta}^{s}}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{{\eta}^{s}}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\Big\rvert\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\Big\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}\Big(\lvert{\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j})}^{i-1}({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}})}\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert{\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c}\beta_{c,i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j})}^{i-1}(1-{(\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j})}^{2})}\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\neq c,c^{*}_{m}}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}-\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}is_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}({{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}w^{*}_{g}})}\rvert+\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}is_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{i}}\rvert\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\Big\rvert\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\Big\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}\Big({p^{*}}^{2}\max_{i}\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},i}}\rvert{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j})}^{k^{*}-1}({w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}})

\displaystyle\quad+{k^{*}}\max_{i}\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c}\beta_{c,k^{*}}}\rvert{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}+{p^{*}}^{2}\max_{c^{\prime},i}\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}}\rvert{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}}

\displaystyle\quad+C{p^{*}}^{2}\max_{c^{\prime},i}\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}}\rvert\max_{c^{\prime}\neq c,c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert^{k^{*}}}(\max_{c^{\prime}\neq c}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}}\rvert}+\max_{c^{\prime}\neq c,c^{*}_{m}}\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j}}\rvert)

\displaystyle\quad+C{p^{*}}^{2}\max_{c^{\prime},i}{\lvert{s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}}\rvert}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}(\lvert{{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{g}}}\rvert+\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta}^{s}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w^{s}_{m,j}}\Big\rvert\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\Big\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}+(1+\frac{1}{3}a_{2})\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\max_{c^{\prime}}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}}\rvert}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}

\displaystyle\quad+A_{3}\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert{{(\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{\eta}^{s}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\Big\rvert\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{0}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\frac{2}{3}a_{2})\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\max_{c^{\prime}}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}}\rvert}\max\Big\{\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1},{(\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}})^{k^{*}-1}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+A_{3}\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}}{(\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}\Big\lvert{\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j}{w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s^{\prime}}}_{w_{m,j}}}\Big\rvert\Big\}.

Since,
{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
c\neq c^{\prime}
,
{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
c
,
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
(c,j)\neq(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
and
\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
j\in[J]
, the term
{k^{*}}\max_{i}\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c}\beta_{c,k^{*}}}\rvert{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}
subsumes the remaining terms within the expression
\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}(\cdot)
with wight
\frac{1}{3}a_{2}
in the fourth inequality.
\frac{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert{{(\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}
are subsumed with weight
\frac{1}{3}a_{2}
by
\eta^{s}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
and
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert\leq A_{2}A_{3}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}
in the fifth inequality.

For the noise term, if
s\leq A_{2}d^{k^{*}-1}
,

\displaystyle\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j}{w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s^{\prime}}}_{w_{m,j}}}\Big\rvert}\leq\eta_{e}A_{1}\sqrt{s}\leq\frac{1}{2}a_{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}

with high probability.

If
s>A_{2}d^{k^{*}-1}
,

\displaystyle\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j}{w^{s^{\prime}}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s^{\prime}}}_{w_{m,j}}}\Big\rvert}\leq\eta_{e}A_{1}\sqrt{s}\leq\frac{{a_{2}\eta_{e}s}}{3CM^{2}}k^{*}\max_{c^{\prime}}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}}\rvert}{(\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}})}^{k^{*}-1}

with high probability.

Thus,

\displaystyle\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}

\displaystyle\leq(1+a_{2})\max\Big\{\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{0}_{c,m,j}}\rvert},\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+a_{2})\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\max_{c^{\prime}}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}}\rvert}\max\Big\{\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c,m,j}}\rvert},Q^{s^{\prime}}_{\text{I},c}\Big\}^{k^{*}-1}

\displaystyle\quad+A_{3}\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}}{(\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

In contrast, the following inequality holds for
Q^{s}_{\text{I},c}
:

\displaystyle Q^{s+1}_{\text{I},c}

\displaystyle\leq(1+a_{2})\max\{\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert},\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}\}+(1+a_{2})\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}\max_{c^{\prime}}{\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},k^{*}}}\rvert}{(Q^{s^{\prime}}_{\text{I},c})}^{k^{*}-1}

\displaystyle+A_{3}\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}}{(\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

Therefore, by induction, we establish that
\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}}\rvert}
is upper bounded by
Q^{s}_{\text{I},c}
for all
j\in\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
and
s=0,1,\ldots,t+1
with high probability.

We apply a similar procedure for
g
and
j\in\mathcal{J}_{m}^{*}
.

\displaystyle\lvert{\kappa^{s+1}_{g,m,j}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\Big\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}\Big[i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}({w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}-{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}{\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})

\displaystyle\quad+i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}s_{c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}(1-{({\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}})}^{2})\Big]+\frac{{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert}{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\Big\rvert\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\Big\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}\Big(\lvert{\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j})}^{i-1}({{w^{*}_{g}}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}-{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}{\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j}})}\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert\sum_{c^{\prime}\neq c^{*}_{m}}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}{i\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j})}^{i-1}({{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}-{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}{\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j}})}\rvert}

\displaystyle\quad+{\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}\sum_{i=k^{*}}^{p^{*}}is_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\gamma_{i}{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{i-1}(1-{(\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j})}^{2})}}\rvert\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\Big\rvert\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},\Big\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}+\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}\Big(k^{*}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j})}^{k^{*}-1}({\lvert{{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}}\rvert}+{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert})

\displaystyle\quad+{p^{*}}^{2}\max_{i}\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},i}}\rvert{(\kappa^{t}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j})}^{k^{*}}({\lvert{{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{*}_{m}}}}\rvert}+{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert})

\displaystyle\quad+C{p^{*}}^{2}\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m},i}\lvert{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}\beta_{c^{\prime},i}}\rvert\max_{c^{\prime}\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{c^{\prime},m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}(\max_{c\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}}\rvert}+\max_{c^{\prime}\neq c^{*}_{m}}{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{c^{\prime},m,j}}\rvert})

\displaystyle\quad+{k^{*}}{\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}{s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\gamma_{k^{*}}}}\rvert}{\lvert{\kappa^{t}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}+C{p^{*}}^{2}\max_{c^{\prime},i}{\lvert{s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,i,c^{\prime}}}\gamma_{i}\rvert}{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert{({\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}+{\eta}^{s}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){{\Xi}^{s}}_{w_{m,j}}\Big\rvert\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{A_{1}\eta_{e},{\lvert{\kappa^{0}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}+(1+\frac{2}{3}a_{2})\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s^{\prime}}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}{s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\gamma_{k^{*}}}}\rvert}{\max\big\{{\lvert{\kappa^{s^{\prime}}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1},{(\frac{1}{2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}})^{k^{*}-1}}\big\}}

\displaystyle\quad+A_{3}\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}k^{*}{\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{*}_{m}}\beta_{c^{*}_{m},k^{*}}}{(\kappa^{s}_{c^{*}_{m},m,j^{*}_{m}})}^{k^{*}-1}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\Big\lvert{\sum_{s^{\prime}=0}^{s}{{\eta}^{s}{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}(I_{d}-w^{s}_{m,j}{w^{s}_{m,j}}^{\top}){\Xi}^{s}_{w_{m,j}}}}\Big\rvert\Big\}.

Since,
{w^{*}_{c}}^{\top}{w^{*}_{c^{\prime}}}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
c\neq c^{\prime}
,
{w^{*}_{g}}^{\top}w^{*}_{c}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
c
,
\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
(c,j)\neq(c^{*}_{m},j^{*}_{m})
and
\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}=\tilde{O}(d^{-\frac{1}{2}})
for all
j\in[J]
, the term
{k^{*}}\max_{i}\lvert{\sum_{c^{\prime}\in[C]}s_{c^{\prime}}\tilde{\alpha}_{m,j,k^{*},c^{\prime}}\gamma_{k^{*}}}\rvert{\lvert{\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}}\rvert}^{k^{*}-1}
subsumes the remaining terms within the expression
\frac{{\eta}^{s}}{CM^{2}}(\cdot)
with weight
\frac{1}{3}a_{2}
in the fourth inequality.
\frac{\lvert\kappa^{s}_{g,m,j}\rvert{{(\eta}^{s})}^{2}{A_{1}}^{2}d}{2}+\frac{{({\eta}^{s})}^{3}{A_{1}}^{3}d^{\frac{3}{2}}}{2}
are also subsumed with weight
\frac{1}{3}a_{2}
by
\eta^{s}\leq a_{4}d^{-\frac{k^{*}}{2}}
and
\lvert\kappa^{s}_{c,m,j}\rvert\leq A_{2}A_{3}d^{-\frac{1}{2}}
in the fourth inequality.
